# `duplication-remediation` reference: `reported duplication and current consolidation boundaries`

## Summary

The 2026-10-02 scratch sweep reports 277 jscpd clones and 1.38 percent duplicated lines against 11 clones and 0.05 percent on 2026-09-04. Clone count increased approximately 25.2 times; the percentage increased 27.6 times. It also reports 4,889 AST candidates, approximately 550 in the meaningful detector families, three vulture leads, and 28 rows containing behavioral divergence candidates. These are reported measurements and leads, not a reproduced post-merge baseline or confirmed defect count.

The supplied report is `C:/Users/hello/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/Y--code-cadrumo-worktrees-tui/9c88c652-3901-431b-84f5-9a24441d2ee0/scratchpad/dup/REPORT.md`. Per-lane evidence is under that directory. The report references raw signals under the sibling scratchpad signals directory; verify actual artifact paths before relying on them.

Read-only orientation on 2026-10-02 found worktree `Y:/code/cadrumo-worktrees/tui`, branch `feature/tui`, and an active merge. Current code search returned usable results with freshness `unverifiable` and non-authoritative absence. Source files were read directly for the observations below. No behavior tests or fresh duplication sweep were run for this design. Every locator needs revalidation after merge and before implementation.

## Existing canonical mechanisms

- `src/cadrumo/application/operations/operation_definition.py:29` defines the existing executor factory, and `:53` defines the operation contract. `operations/capabilities.py:64` owns capability validation. Repeated registration construction does not justify another operation platform.
- `src/cadrumo/application/operations/models.py:181` owns terminal receipt meaning. The live failure-code lookup block at approximately `:191` is repeated; projector checks should only retain facts the receipt model cannot know.
- Runtime frontends have distinct orchestration contracts. `entrypoints/cli/runtime_registered_operation.py:427` blocks toward settlement and handles review; `entrypoints/tui/operations/runtime_controller.py:99` preserves async observation and cancellation; `src/cadrumo_harness/mcp/admitted_operations.py:100` submits and starts while observation/results remain separate calls. Consolidate equal exchange mechanisms without forcing one lifecycle onto all frontends.
- `src/cadrumo/core/decimal/grammar.py:68` owns strict human decimal parsing. Its signed and precision parameters carry real policy. `src/cadrumo/core/parsing/dates.py:74` is an optional permissive parser, while `:109` is its required extended-form counterpart. Selecting the strict contract matters as much as selecting the defining module.
- `src/cadrumo/domain/calculations/registry/facts/resolution.py` owns typed governed-fact queries and provenance-bearing resolution. Repeated payload flatteners may share structural validation, but cannot silently homogenize stripping, key filters, date selection, aliases or fallback.
- `src/cadrumo/domain/calculations/registry/binding_provider_registration.py:226` declares SUM and COPY for a fold operation set. Live SUM-only validators include `ledger_oss_bindings.py:198`, `ledger_iva_bindings.py:827`, `ledger_renta_income_bindings.py:176` and `ledger_impatriado_bindings.py:178`. Registration and validator disagree; this does not establish which support contract is correct.
- `src/cadrumo/core/async_cleanup.py` already owns cleanup failure traversal. Extend its owned behavior for composition if needed instead of importing a private helper or repeating one-level attachment merging.
- `dev/audit/duplication.py:87` pins jscpd 4.2.0. Its standing scope is the product package and its outcomes distinguish observed zero, clones and unavailable. `dev/audit/semantic_duplication.py:471` exposes seven candidate detectors. Scan counts are advisory; semantic contract gates provide acceptance.

Verified S27 partial migration now uses `application/operations/profile_guard.py::require_operation_profile` in 32 operation consumer modules (six initial consumers and 26 Modelo consumers). It enforces expected request target, executor definition/subject identity and active profile with `PROFILE_MISMATCH`; an explicit expected subject preserves WorkUnit targeting. Expected definition, spreadsheet exact-type admission, Quickfile optional payload bucket, and later repository/worker/commit checks remain at their original boundaries. The combined owning selection passed 304 tests; final Quickfile policy-helper and guard-docstring changes passed their 22-test subset with scoped static checks. This is partial family acceptance. Attestation/review-package error/timing variants, threaded LLM admission and retained-port reads require separate dispositions; remaining compatible consumers still need migration. The live query reader previously omitted executor-definition parity and now refuses that mismatch before work.

S27 now defines two explicit contracts in `application/operations/profile_guard.py`: `require_profile_operation_identity` checks the profile/WorkUnit request target and executor definition/subject without reading the active pointer; `require_operation_profile` delegates that identity contract and additionally requires the active profile. Nine retained-port ledger readers use the pure contract after their local expected-definition check. LLM diagnostics keeps caller-thread identity admission, its pre-phase `to_thread` active-profile lookup and later worker active check. Ledger writes use the strong contract at their original admission/read/commit boundaries; the duplicate export/link profile helper is deleted. Owning tests prove typed early refusals, no phase/provider work on mismatch, and exact retained bucket/operation without a new active-pointer gate.

The combined owning selection collected 474 cases: 470 passed and four failed only because external invoice-evidence decomposition moved the test's authority-legends patch target. The reserved test-only correction imports actual extract definitions and patches its current lookup; the four-case rerun passed with effect/consent/custody assertions intact. The unchanged 470 results are reused only for unchanged dependencies. Query-read was excluded during S08 changes, and its concurrently changed wallet factories/tests require S08's current receipt. Fifteen slice paths and the corrected sixteenth test pass scoped Ruff/format/type checks; all sixteen final hashes matched architect reread. Current external extract/confirm/readiness modules were read-only and still call the canonical guard; their receipts match the owner's split handoff. S27 stays open for residual guards and complete family disposition.

## Security findings need reachability evidence

The core sensitive-key terms at `src/cadrumo/core/redaction/rules.py:80` include authorization, certificate, NIE, NIF and tax_id. `src/cadrumo/core/errors/error_codes.py:36` repeats a narrower key pattern, and `:333` uses it before value redaction. The public envelope at `:367` receives that result. Value redaction may still remove recognizable values. Test opaque synthetic sensitive values under the missed keys through JSON and text rendering before asserting exposure.

`src/cadrumo/core/remote_authority.py:21` enforces HTTPS. `adapters/outbound/aeat/sede/_adapter_utils.py:60` classifies auth-gate redirects without that scheme check. Its callers include `nif_iva_check.py:437` and `iva_compensation_wallet.py:158`. This is a real classification difference; unsafe request acceptance is not yet established.

`adapters/outbound/storage/_google_drive.py:109` admits an apostrophe in a namespace, and `:599` interpolates the namespace into a single-quoted query. `adapters/outbound/google/drive_entries.py:81` already defines query-literal escaping. Untrusted namespace ingress has not been established. Pure escaping can move atomically to an inward defining module such as the expected new `core/google_drive_query.py`; Google ownership selection and SDK behavior remain adapter-owned. Reusing a concrete sibling adapter solely as a helper is not the boundary solution.

## Representative divergence observations

- Bare Decimal and date constructors remain in `entrypoints/tui/ledger/invoice_entry.py:227`, `:275`, `:288` and `actividad_asset.py:48`, `:195`. Frontend admission parity needs strict parsing with the appropriate units and optionality.
- `application/aggregation/atribucion_member.py:379` recognizes fewer boolean tokens than `core/parsing/utils.py:35`; invalid/absent must stay distinct from false.
- `core/identity/documents.py:404` and `core/identity/nif_iva.py:77` normalize punctuation differently. `domain/contribuyente/descendant_record.py:496` performs shape normalization without the same checksum proof. Separate shape, normalization and validated identity.
- `domain/calculations/registry/schema_scalars.py:272` does not strip dots, while `sede/nif_iva_check.py:613` uses the canonical NIF-IVA normalization.
- `core/aeat_csv.py:20` carries cap 32; `adapters/inbound/borrador/_extractors/modelo_100_summary_v2025.py:50` uses cap 24.
- `application/aggregation/retenciones.py:71` and `application/modelo/invoice_withholding_capture_public.py:165` carry digit-only province shapes unlike the canonical 01-52 province contract.
- Certificate source bounds differ between `application/auth/models.py:27` and `application/auth/certificate_source_operation.py:91`.
- Drive ID lengths differ in `google/document_link_resolver.py:53` and `application/ledger/attachment_review.py:18`. The resolver explicitly documents URL-context admission; adjudicate context before changing either.
- `application/aggregation/source_mesh.py:1003` builds a row-coordinate mapping that can overwrite; `domain/modelos/calculation_revision.py:1060` rejects duplicate coordinates.
- `adapters/persistence/storage/custody/_kdf_codec.py:120` lacks a zero-progress write guard; `storage/custody/filesystem.py:331` retries PermissionError broadly. Verify both via controlled I/O conditions.
- `entrypoints/tui/modelo/workbench/navigator.py:243` fits by codepoint length. `entrypoints/tui/runtime_management.py:253` presents Confirm before Cancel without explicit decline focus; prove default focus in a real widget test.
- `application/modelo/work_plazo.py:155` resolves deadline posture separately from the shifted close-date path in `work_form_service.py:168`. Preserve the difference between nominal deadline, effective deadline and conditional recargo preview until its use is adjudicated.
- `dev/quality/write_path_coverage.py:128` assigns findings and tool error the same exit code. `dev/locales/wizard_translation_audit.py:163` approximates relative import resolution. These require process-outcome and AST fixtures respectively.

## Scope and hazards

Existing active work includes complexity-remediation, semantic-consolidation, binding-consumer-closure, tui-architecture and mcp-purpose-authentication. A closed Step is orientation, not proof of present correctness. Reconcile each proposed write with its current owner before assignment.

The supplied report reserves registry loader semantics and development scan-scope definitions to tui-bb. Leave their code and adjudication to that owner; consume current policy rather than duplicate it.

Do not merge equal-looking values across meanings: fraction versus percent IVA rates, prorrata versus bienes direction, domain versus core period, optional versus required date parsing, active-bucket versus admission identity, evidence SHA-256 versus cache/change fingerprints, distinct Argon2 unit regimes, nominal versus business-day-shifted deadlines, or independent financial test oracles. Locale text equality does not prove semantic key identity.

## Current filing dispositions

These are source-level observations, not behavioral or legal acceptance. The source reads narrow the supplied nine filing rows:

- Row 4: `src/cadrumo/application/aggregation/oss_ioss.py:483` receives devengo, but `:506` omits the date when resolving the tier. `src/cadrumo/domain/invoices/enums.py:561` defaults it to the current Madrid date.
- Row 5: `domain/invoices/validators.py:41` and `domain/transactions/models.py:831` expose different XI treatment. This needs supply-, territory-, date- and modelo-specific evidence; a universal EU boolean is not justified.
- Row 6: `application/aggregation/iva_ledger.py:1781` chooses the first candidate, unlike the exact-one check at `adapters/persistence/profile/transactions.py:323`. Confirm the live defining paths and actual ambiguity before repair.
- Row 7 is likely mis-scoped. The generic roll-forward verification predicate's sole located declaration is M200 BIN stock at `src/cadrumo/_data/registry/aeat/modelos/200/revisions/2024/verification_predicates/0001-declarations.toml:93`. M303 `domain/iva_compensation/filed_derivation.py:56` accepts `refunded` and has refund-versus-carry tests. No live M303 use of the generic predicate was located. Keep the row for a verified rejection, not a duplicate compensation fix.
- Row 8 remains partial: `adapters/persistence/profile/modelos_calculation.py:102` accepts optional taxpayer identity, and `entrypoints/adapter_composition.py:1614` creates a repository without it. The reported ten-bundle count and rectificativa reachability need a complete live inventory.
- Row 9: `application/modelo/verification_predicates.py:104` explicitly maps absent values to Decimal zero. Predicate-specific completeness requirements must be adjudicated before changing defaults.
- Row 10: `domain/iva_compensation/carry_forward.py:551` uses literal four, while `balance.py:121` resolves a governed fact currently equal to four. This is authority duplication and future drift risk; no current numeric mismatch was demonstrated.
- Row 11: `domain/modelos/calculation_revision_identity.py:77` uses `str(normalize())`, while `domain/identifiers.py:61` defines the canonical fixed-point identity representation. Assess existing persisted identity effects before migration.
- Row 12: `application/calculations/bienes_inversion_regularizacion.py:157` selects a casilla by name tokens. `domain/calculations/registry/prorrata_regularizacion_bindings.py:32` already owns typed prorrata source coordinates; prove the revision/source match before reuse.

Further S08 source reread changes the severity interpretation of report row 8: `domain/modelos/calculation_revision_aggregate.py:77` explicitly refuses an M303 rectificativa when `expected_taxpayer_tax_id` is absent, and compares the justificante taxpayer/coordinate when present. `adapters/persistence/profile/modelos_calculation.py:183` supplies that aggregate context during real catalogue deserialization. The missing composition argument therefore does not establish a bypass in the current tree; it can block supported reads and must still be reconciled per live bundle. Current omitted constructor candidates include participation-index rebuild, IVA-wallet seed and filing-action bundles in `entrypoints/adapter_composition.py`, plus justificante, ledger-action and live-state compositions. These omissions can refuse valid stored rectificativa reads. These remain source dispositions pending S08 owning positive/mismatch/absence tests. S05 authority comparison narrows rows 4 and 6. ### Remaining S06 family dispositions

F08 is a confirmed duplicate binding-ID literal in `application/modelo/iva_wallet_gate.py`: the defining public constant is `MODELO_303_IVA_COMPENSATION_BINDING_ID` in `domain/calculations/registry/iva_wallet_carry_targets.py`, already imported directly by binding prefill. A one-file direct-import cleanup is technically sufficient; literal test oracles should remain independent. The write stays held because the external S07 casilla-consumer reservation is broad and its exact overlap cannot be certified.

J05 repeats the next-year December-31 date primitive in transaction candidate-span selection, cash payment validation and residual-settlement emission. Those caller roles are distinct and must remain local: a candidate date span is not a posted settlement. A shared domain date primitive is proposed, with boundary and indexed/full-scan/residual tests; implementation remains held on the aggregation/input overlap. Statutory locator supplied by the owner: LIVA articles 163 terdecies(1),(3)(a) and 163 quinquiesdecies(1), `https://boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740&p=20260930&tn=1`.

J10 has contextual dispositions rather than a mechanical period replacement. The canonical pinned M303 settlement helper resolves declared quarterly/monthly terminal periods and already has 4T/12 and refusal evidence. The two prior-year source fences intentionally retain both 4T and 12, including absence; any shared candidate helper would require complete pinned read/commit threading and remains held with the atomic caller migration. The accepted cross-period-prorrata ADR's settlement section explicitly uses 4T/0A, the current settle-sector payload has no period/cadence axis, and the public guide describes fourth-quarter settlement. Retain that quarterly-shaped operation and its literal independent tests under current scope. Adding monthly settle-sector support would be a separate public-payload/support decision, not an implied dedup requirement. The quarterly annual partition and 4T-to-M390 summary retain their explicit scopes.

J17 is a local wrapper pair whose five callers all supply one semantic CasillaId. A direct mapping lookup preserves absent None versus present Decimal zero; the one-file cleanup and history/validated-envelope tests remain held until S08/S06 releases the live dependency.

J19 is a verified no-write disposition: five application modules import canonical compensation casilla constants under local vocabulary aliases. They copy neither values nor behavior and expose no public forwarding surface. Renaming those aliases would not remove a second source of truth. J07 direction-dependent signs likewise remain contextual. These findings do not close the still-pending carry caller work or S06.

### S18 and S20 source-backed design prerequisites

S18 N03/C11: all seven ledger binding validators and their aggregation callbacks are SUM-only, while provider registration advertises SUM/COPY for scalar ledger output. This is a registration/validator contract contradiction; it is not authority to implement ledger COPY. Accepted binding-schema and calculation-aggregation decisions retain COPY for non-ledger/carry providers. A proposed ledger-specific allowed-operation authority and shared defining validation primitive must preserve row-channel behavior and each family's error/selector/provenance policy. IRNR's scalar-and-rows declaration versus current scalar/SUM-only paths is a separate unresolved lead. The standalone published-binding census was unavailable because that invocation had no configured authority descriptor/root; this supplies no census and is not itself a product defect. Reuse the configured published-authority test artifact for actual evidence. Existing provider-diagnostic and ledger IVA/Renta diffs must be owned before any migration; no S18 write is accepted.

S20 B03/M07: calculate advisory still counts overdue from the nominal close, while work-form and overview count from the shifted effective filing close. A real published M136 2023 4T fixture has nominal 2024-01-20 Saturday and effective 2024-01-22 Monday; an injected 2024-01-21 reference date discriminates posture and the rate-only preview. The reusable mechanism should be an application-owned facts projection of an already-resolved close, explicit reference date, explicitly declared optional holiday territory and the exact held authority operation. It delegates the defining filing-window and holiday-shift authorities; overview supplies its already-applicable obligation rather than resolving another one. Preserve typed qualifiers, coverage/shift reason, names/jurisdictions and annual BOE provenance. Separate M210 domiciliacion payment cutoff from filing close and keep post-calculation result/tipo qualifiers in their own resolver. No new filing/monthly support or authority dates are introduced.

Decision coverage: `2026-08-24-deadline-window-revision-authority-adr` retains semantic filing year, exact declared windows, explicit reference date and source fidelity; `2026-09-23-calendar-obligations-holiday-jurisdiction-adr` permits verified regional shift only for explicitly declared common-regime resident territory and requires distinct national-only, territory-unverified, not-shifted and unavailable states. Municipal holidays remain unevaluated and must be visible. The calculate/advisory path currently lacks territory input; it must use the already-loaded profile's canonical typed projection under the same operation, or claim national-only scope explicitly. Extending public snapshot/payload fields to preserve nominal/effective/coverage requires its exact coverage and caller migration review before code.

The S20 proposed complete migration overlaps S08 work_form_service and externally dirty work-plazo, advisory, operation/CLI/TUI and holiday-definition files. Clean calendar/form model files alone do not make the whole migration disjoint. No S20 implementation is released. Its existing meaningful oracles include M136 date/count parity, regional versus national/unverified coverage, unavailable calendar, M369 no-shift, effective recovery boundaries and the separate M210 payment window.

## Prepared filing grounding

These references support the next source investigation; they do not establish completion or runtime adoption.

- LIVA article 90.Dos, official consolidated text at `https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-1992-28740#a90`, inspected 2026-10-02, makes the applicable Spanish rate depend on devengo. The authored slot catalogue `src/cadrumo/_data/registry/aeat/facts/0094-iva-rate-slot-catalogue.toml:7` already selects its variant on `devengo_date`; S05 must pass the invoice's resolved date through the real published resolver rather than the observation date. Do not apply Spanish pricing authority to a foreign destination.
- Real Decreto-ley 4/2024 article 1, official original text at `https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2024-12944`, inspected 2026-10-02, separates the July-September and October-December 2024 food-rate windows. Candidate ambiguity still needs a real published-table case and supply context; coexisting numeric rates within one tier are not necessarily multiple distinct tiers.
- AEAT `Manual práctico IVA 2021`, printed page 8, `https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/static_files/Sede/Biblioteca/Manual/Practicos/IVA/ManualIVA2021.pdf`, inspected 2026-10-02, distinguishes Northern Ireland goods treatment from services beginning 2021-01-01. The manual's source-year is preserved. AEAT Modelo 349 instructions at `https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/static_files/Sede/Procedimiento_ayuda/GI28/instr_mod_349.pdf` separately describe XI and exclude services keys. This is territory/context evidence, not proof of Modelo 369 rate-table coverage. The current product deliberately accepts XI as a goods prefix while the IVA rate table lacks XI; S05 must distinguish unsupported destination authority from a silent omitted supply.

## Hazards and reserved ownership

Do not collapse these merely because names/values are similar:

- Report same-name/meaning hazards: IVA rate is a fraction in Transaction/Invoice, percent in wizard/draft; TUI wizard and domain _validate_iva_rate use opposite units. Regularización direction sign differs between prorrata and bienes. domain/period and core/period are separate. Three _parse_iso_date, four _require_profile meanings, two repository_files functions and load_revision vs _load_revision are not presumed equal. Argon2 KiB and MiB regimes are deliberately distinct. blake2b_hex change discriminators are not evidence hashes.
- Runtime/operation: active bucket, subject, context identity, grant, and admission are separate checks; frontend submit/observe lifecycles differ (CLI review/settlement, TUI async cursor/cancel, MCP separate calls). Keep operation-specific capabilities, disclosure category, scope, REVIEW/RESPOND, effects, and identity boundaries explicit.
